_July, 2011_

HX8282-A01(A) 1536CH TFT LCD Source Driver with LVDS TCON

_July, 2011_

## Preliminary Version 01

## **1. General Description**

HX8282-A01 is a 1536-channel outputs source driver with LVDS, TCON, and 3-wire Serial Port Interface. This chip integrates 1536ch dual gate mode source driver with LVDS and parallel RGB input interface. The TCON generates the 1024x600, 1024x768, 800x600, 800x480 and 960x540 resolutions and provides horizontal and vertical control timing to source driver and gate driver.

It also supports dithering feature, apply source driver with 6-bit DAC to perform 8-bit resolution 256 gray scales. The source driver receives 6-bit by 3 dots of digital display data per clock from TCON and generates corresponding 64-level gray scale voltage output. Since the output circuit of this source driver incorporates an operational amplifier with low power dissipation, and performs wide voltage supply range and small output deviation. Therefore, a high quality display with less crosstalk can be achieved.

_July, 2011_

HX8282-A01(A)

1536CH TFT LCD Source Driver with LVDS TCON

_DATA SHEET Preliminary V01_

## **2. Features**

## **TCON**

- Support display resolution 1024 RGB x 600, 1024 RGB x768, 800RGBx600, 800RGBx480 and 960RGBx540.

- Support LVDS/TTL interface

- Support single or dual gate function

- Support cascade function with bidirectional shift control (CMOS signal)

- Internal dithering 8-bit data to 6-bit data for Source Driver Circuit( 6-bit DAC + 2-bit FRC or HFRC)

**==> picture [33 x 33] intentionally omitted <==**

- Built-In CABC function

- Built-In AUTO pattern

- Built-In SDRRS function

- Only support stripe types of panel group

**==> picture [117 x 98] intentionally omitted <==**

- Operation frequency: 71 MHz max

- Provide source and gate drivers control timing

- Provide flip and mirror scan control

- Operation Voltage Level 2.3V to 3.6V

## **Source Driver**

- 1536 channels output source driver for TFT LCD panel

- Embedded Gamma Table for special custom request

- V1~V14 for adjusting Gamma correction

- Dynamic output range: 0.1 to AVDD-0.1V

- Voltage deviation of outputs: ±20mV

- Dot inversion and 1+2 Dot inversion driving scheme

- Right and left shift capability

**==> picture [31 x 32] intentionally omitted <==**

- LCD power: 6.5 to 13.5V

**==> picture [33 x 32] intentionally omitted <==**

## **Power**

- Built-In PWM controller for AVDD , VGH / VGL , and VCOM buffer

- Support Half voltage source driver

## **Application**

- Special designed for low cost UMPC application

## **Others**

- COG package

## **4. Pin Description**

## **4.1 Pin description**

|**Name**|**I/O**|**Description**|**Description**||
|---|---|---|---|---|
|D07~D00<br>D17~D10<br>D27~D20|I|LVDS or Parallel RGB data input. Select|||
|||Inputmode|TTL|LVDS|
|||IFSEL|L|H|
|||D20,D21|B0,B1|D0N,D0P|
|||D22,D23|B2,B3|D1N,D1P|
|||D24,D25|B4,B5|D2N,D2P|
|||D26,D27|B6,B7|D3N,D3P|
|DCLK|I<br> <br>|In TTL mode, DCLK for clock input.<br>In LVDS mode , PINC for Positive LVDS differential clock input<br>IFSEL<br>Inputmode<br>Description<br>L<br>TTL<br>DCLK<br>H<br>LVDS<br>PINC|||
|||IFSEL|Inputmode|Description|
|||L|TTL|DCLK|
|||H|LVDS|PINC|
|NINC|I|Negative LVDS differential clock input.|||
|VSD|I|In TTL mode, for Vertical Sync input. Negative polarity.<br>In LVDS mode. Connected to FPC and pull low.|||
|HSD|I|In TTL mode, for Horizontal Sync input. Negative polarity.<br>In LVDS mode, used as 6-bit/8-bit input select.<br>HSD<br>Bit<br>L<br>8-bit<br>H<br>6-bit|||
|DEN|I|Data input enable for TTL mode. Active high to enable the data<br>input. Normally pull low.|||
|MODE|I|DE / SYNC mode select. Normally pull high.<br>When MODE=H, DE mode.<br>When MODE=L, SYNC mode.|||
|SCL|I|Input reference clock for serial interface. Data is fed at the rising<br>edge of the clock. Normally pull low.|||
|SDA|I/O|Input for data of serial interface. Normally pull low.|||
|CSB|I|Input the chip select signal for serial interface. Normally pull low.<br>Low: Select. (Enable)<br>High: Non-select. (Disable)|||
|IFSEL|I|TTL / LVDS interface selection. Normally pull low.<br>When IFSEL=L, TTL interface.<br>When IFSEL=H, LVDS interface.|||
|DITHER|I|Dithering function enable control. Normally pull low.<br>When DITHER=H, Enable internal dithering function.<br>When DITHER=L,Disable internal ditheringfunction.|||

HX8282-A01(A)

1536CH TFT LCD Source Driver with LVDS TCON

_DATA SHEET Preliminary V01_

|**Name**|**I/O**||**Description**|**Description**|**Description**|**Description**|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|HFRC|I||H-FRC selection. Normally pull low.<br>When HFRC=H: H-FRC enable.<br>When HFRC=L: FRC enable.<br>If DITHER=L , disable dithering function.(HFRC and FRC disable)||||
|RESL[2:0]|I||Display resolution selection.<br>RESL2<br>RESL1<br>RESL0<br>Resolution<br>0<br>0<br>0<br>1024(RGB)x600 (Default)<br>0<br>0<br>1<br>1024(RGB)x768<br>0<br>1<br>0<br>800(RGB)x600 (601~936 disable)<br>0<br>1<br>1<br>800(RGB)x480 (601~936 disable)<br>1<br>x<br>x<br>960(RGB)x540 (721~816 disable)||||
||||RESL2|RESL1|RESL0|Resolution|
||||0|0|0|1024(RGB)x600 (Default)|
||||0|0|1|1024(RGB)x768|
||||0|1|0|800(RGB)x600 (601~936 disable)|
||||0|1|1|800(RGB)x480 (601~936 disable)|
||||1|x|x|960(RGB)x540 (721~816 disable)|
|GRB|I||Global reset. KeepVDD duringoperation. Normally pull high.||||
|DCLKPOL|I||Define input clock polarity. Normally pull low.<br>When DCLKPOL=H, latch data by rising edge of CLK.<br>When DCLKPOL=L, latch data by falling edge of CLK.<br>In LVDS mode , please connect to L.||||
|DUAL|I||Dual gate function enables control. Normally pull high.<br>DUAL=H, enable dual gate function.<br>DUAL=L, disable dual gate function.<br>**Note:** Cascadefunction willbe disabled under “dualgate” mode!||||
|V1~V14|I||When INTERNAL Gamma Table is used. GAMH tied to AVDDG via<br>resistor for PWR_EN=H enable PWM, or tied to AVDD for<br>PWR_EN=L disable PWM.<br>When using external gamma voltage, GAMH and GAML are<br>floating, and V1~V14 are the external gamma correction points.<br>The voltage of these pins must be:<br>AGND<V14<V13<V12<V11<V10<V9<V8;<br>V7<V6<V5<V4<V3<V2<V1<AVDD.||||
|GAMH|I||When using INTERNAL Gamma Table, GAMH tied to AVDDG via<br>resistor for PWR_EN=H enable PWM, or tied to AVDD for<br>PWR_EN=L disable PWM. Otherwise floating.||||
|GAML|I||When using INTERNAL Gamma Table, tied to GND. Otherwise<br>floating.||||
|STBYB|I||Standby mode control. Normally pull High.<br>When STB=H, all the functions are on. (Default pulls high)<br>When STB=L, TCON and source driver are off and all output are<br>GND.||||
|MASL|I||Master and slave mode selection in cascade mode. Normally pull<br>high.<br>MASL=H, for Master mode.(Default)<br>MASL=L, for Slave mode.<br>Only the Master chip will issue the Gate and Cascade control<br>signal.||||
|MASLOC|I||Master location definition pin. Normally pull low.<br>MASLOC=L, master locate on right side. (Panel top view) (Default)<br>MASLOC=H, master locate on left side. (Panel top view)||||
|SHLR|I||Source Driver internal shift register is controlled by this pin as<br>shown below: Normally pull high.<br>SHLR=H: SO1�SO2�SO3�‧‧‧�SO1536. (Default)<br>SHLR=L: SO1536�SO1535�SO1534�‧‧‧�SO1.||||

_July, 2011_

HX8282-A01(A)

1536CH TFT LCD Source Driver with LVDS TCON

_DATA SHEET Preliminary V01_

|**Name**|**I/O**|**Description**|
|---|---|---|
|UPDN|I|Gate Driver Up/down scan setting. Normally pull low.<br>When UPDN=H, reverse scan.<br>STV1 output vertical start pulse and UD pin output “H” to Gate<br>driver<br>When UPDN=L, normal scan. (Default)<br>STV2 output vertical start pulse and UD pin output “L” to Gate<br>driver.|
|BIST|I|Normal Operation/BIST pattern select. Normally pull low.<br>When BIST=H, BIST.(CLK input is not needed)<br>When BIST=L, Normal Operation. (Default)|
|NBW|I|Normally black or normally white setting. Normally pull low.<br>When NBW=H, Normally black.<br>When NBW=L, Normally white. (Default)|
|REV|I|Controls whether the data of D00~D27 are inverted or not. Normally<br>pull low.<br>When REV=H, the data will be inverted.<br>EX.“00”�“3F”,“07”� “38”,“15”�“2A”, and so on.|
|FRAME|I|Frame inverse or not select. Normally pull low.<br>When FRAME=H, Uniform.<br>When FRAME=L, Frame inverse. (Default)|
|SEL[1:0]|I|Gate on sequence select. Normally pull low.<br>SEL[1]<br>SEL[0]<br>Pin control function<br>0<br>0<br>Z<br>0<br>1<br>弓<br>1<br>0<br>1<br>1<br>Z+弓|
|OEVR/OEVL|O|Gate driver control signal.|
|SYNC1R/SYNC1L|<br>O|Gate driver control signal.(CABC and BIST sync control)|
|SYNC2R/SYNC2L|<br>O|Gate driver control signal.(CABC and BIST sync control)|
|UDR/UDL|O|Gate driver control signal.|
|CKVR/CKVL|O|Gate driver control signal.|
|STV1R/STV1L|O|Gate driver control signal.|
|STV2R/STV2L|O|Gate driver control signal.|
|STBNR/STBNL|O|Gate driver control signal.|
|F_CtrlR/F_CtrlL|O|Gate driver control signal. (For special Gate on sequence)<br>In Cascade, let this pin floating.<br>In dual gate, connect the pin to gate driver’s F_Ctrl and setting<br>SEL[1:0]=00.|
|CABC_EN[1:0]|I|CABC H/W enable pin. Normally pull low.<br>When CABC_EN=”00”, CABC off. (Default mode)<br>When CABC_EN=”01”, user interface Image.<br>When CABC_EN=”10”, still Picture.<br>When CABC_EN=”11”, moving Image.|
|DIMI|I|Brightness control signal. Normally pull high.|
|DIMO|O|Backlight dimmer signal for external controller.<br>DIMO=H, Logical control signal to turn on external backlight<br>controller.<br>DIMO=L, Turn off external backlight controller.<br>**Note:**If CABC off,DIMO=DIMI. Else DIMO is controlled byCABC.|

_July, 2011_

HX8282-A01(A)

1536CH TFT LCD Source Driver with LVDS TCON

_DATA SHEET Preliminary V01_

|**Name**|**I/O**|**Description**|
|---|---|---|
|PINCTL|I|Enable pin control function. Normally pull high.<br>When PINCTL=L, disable pin control function.<br>When PINCTL=H, enable pin control function.<br>**Note**: The related 3-wire control register bit control will be disabled under.<br>PINCTL=H.|
|OPDRV|I|Source OP driving selection. Normally pull low.<br>OPDRV=H: 140%.<br>OPDRV=L: Normal.|
|AVDD|PI|Analog power.|
|AGND|PI|Analog ground.|
|VDD|PI|Digitalpower.|
|GND|PI|Digitalground.|
|VDD_LVDS|PI|LVDSpower.|
|GND_LVDS|PI|LVDSground.|
|AVDDL|PI|Power supply for Half AVDD application.<br>Connected to AVDD when full range.<br>Connected to Half AVDD when Half AVDD application.|
|AGNDH|PI|Power supply for Half AVDD application.<br>Connected to AGND when full range.<br>Connected to Half AVDD when Half AVDD application.|
|PWR_EN|I|POWER enable. Normally pull low.<br>PWR_EN=H, enable PWM, charge pump and VCOM buffer.<br>PWR_EN=L, disable PWM, charge pump and VCOM buffer.|
|FBA|I|PWM controller feedback input.(for AVDD)|
|DRVA|O|PWM output driver signal for the boost converter.(for AVDD)|
|FBH|I|PWM controller feedback input.(for VGH)|
|DRVH|O|PWM output driver signal for the boost converter.(for VGH)|
|FBL|I|PWM controller feedback input.(for VGL)|
|DRVL|O|PWM output driver signal for the boost converter.(for VGL)|
|DRVL_B|O|Inverse of DRVL.(for VGL)|
|VCOMI|I|VCOM buffer in.|
|VCOMO|O|VCOM buffer out.|
|SO1~SO1536|O|Output driver signal.|
|COM1_IN<br>COM1_OUT|S|Internal link together between input side and output side.|
|COM2_IN<br>COM2_OUT|S|Internal link together between input side and output side.|
|TP|T|Testpins. Theymust be open.|
|SHIELDING|SH|IC Shielding pads.<br>Those pins are internally connected to the AGND.<br>DO NOT connect to any WOA on the panel.|
|DASHD|SH|Data Bus Shielding pad.<br>Those pins are internally connected to the GND.<br>RECOMMAND to add shielding lines on the FPC to reduce EMI.|
|DUM|D|Dummy pads.<br>Thosepins are floating pads.|



**Note: I:** Input, **O** ： Output, **I/O** ： Input/Output, **PI** ： Power input, **PO** ： Power Output, **T** ： Testing, **SH** ： Shielding, **D** ： Dummy, **S:** Shorted line

## **5. The BIST Pattern For Aging Mode Test**

## **5.1 The BIST pattern for aging mode test**

HX8282-A01 supports BIST pattern for aging mode test automatically. When external BIST pin set to “H” level, then HX8282-A01 will leave normal operation mode and starts to generate the BIST pattern to LCD panel without external clock signal. The BIST pattern is illustrated as below:

**==> picture [480 x 391] intentionally omitted <==**

**----- Start of picture text -----**<br>
1  2  3<br>Red  Green  Blue<br>4  5  6<br>Black  White  Flicker<br>7<br>Black with white frame<br>**----- End of picture text -----**<br>


**Table 5.1: BIST mode pattern**

## **7. 3-wire Serial Peripheral Interface (SPI)**

## **7.1 SPI format**

The HX8282-A01 supports the 3-pin serial peripheral interface (SPI) to set internal register. The data is written to the register of assigned address when “End of transfer” is detected after the 16th SCL rising cycles.

Data is not accepted if there are less or more than 16 cycles for one transaction. Only when SCL is input 16 times and CSB is in the "Low" period simultaneously, SDA is accepted. It needs DCLK input for SDA setting.

The first 6 bits (A5 ~ A0) specify the address of the register. The 7th bit means Read/Write command. “0” is WRITE. “1” is READ. The 8[th] bit means “Don’t care or Hi-Z“. It is “Don’t care” in write format, and “Hi-Z” in read format. And the last 8 bits are for Data setting (D7 ~D0). The address and data are transferred from the MSB to LSB sequentially. And next cycle is turn-round cycle.

Setting of all the SPI will take effect at the coming falling edge of VSD except GRB and STB bit.

## **8. Power On/Off Sequence**

To prevent the device damage from latch up, the power on/off sequence shown below must be followed.

Power on: VDD, GND � AVDD, AGND � V1 to V14 Power off: V1 to V14 � AVDD, AGND� VDD, GND

## **8.1 Power on/off control**

HX8282-A01 has a power on/off sequence control function. In order to prevent IC from power on reset fail, the rising time (TPOR) of the digital power supply VDD should be maintained within the given specifications. Please refer to “AC Characteristics” for more detail on timing.

## **11.2.9 SDRRS timing diagram**

SDRRS (Seamless Display Refresh Rate Switching)

When showing the still picture, it is accept to reduce the refresh rate from 60Hz to low refresh rate (for example 40Hz). The purpose is mainly for power saving. INTEL defined a timing chart switch between different refresh rates. Following this timing chart, the switch between different refresh rates is seamless for end user.

**==> picture [481 x 207] intentionally omitted <==**

**----- Start of picture text -----**<br>
T4<br>T2 T3<br>LVDS Clock differential<br>Clock for Refresh Rate 1 Clock Refresh Rate 2<br>Pair held at logical 0 value<br>LVDS Clock differential<br>Data at Clock Rate 1 Data Refresh Rate 2<br>Pair held at logical 0 value<br>T1<br>Last Active Line First Active Line<br>at Refresh Rate 1 at Refresh Rate 2<br>Figure 11.10: SDRRS timing diagram<br>**----- End of picture text -----**<br>


T1-Min delay from start of vert blank to start of timing change: 2 lines T2-Max delay for clock to transition to new frequency: 40μs~100μs T3-Max receiver lock delay from stable clock: 100μs T4-Max period during which panel maintains display: T2+T3

**==> picture [33 x 32] intentionally omitted <==**

**==> picture [87 x 92] intentionally omitted <==**
